B. Number of objects, size, shape, colours, brightness, sound, smell ete.

‘D. Naked eye, binoculars, other optical device, still or movie camera.

speed,

J. Telephone lines; high voltage lines, reservoir, lake or damg swamp or
marsh; river, high buildings, tall chimneys, steeples, spires, TV or
radio masts; airfields, generating plant; factories, pits or other
Sites with flood-lights or other night lighting. |

Driving northbound on M3, just past the M25
junction. 'UFO' was about two miles away on the
right. Seen with naked eye.

First assumption was that it was a warning light
on a building or mast so, several hundred feet.

Hovered for 60 secs then flew eastwards at very
high speed.

Slightly misty with generally low cloud cover.

No, only work colleagues.

No-one specific although the M3 was very busy so

sighting was made at Manchester Airport the same

Nevada names
lonely highway for
strange sightings
Pe teccwisd Pres

RACHEL, Nev. — If ET. is ever
looking for a place to phone home,
or searching for a route back to his
extraterrestrial kin, this tiny blip of
a town may be just the ticket.

Long the mecca for sci-fi faithful
who believe that we are not alone,
Rachel is now the anchor for Neva-
da's newest attraction: the Extra-
terrestrial Highway.

Folks here are convinced visi-
tors from outer space live just over
the mountains to the south, at a top-
secret government base known as
Area 51 or Groom Lake.

“There are people and machines
from other planets over there,” Pat
Travis said as she scrubbed break-

fast dishes at the Little A’Le'Inn — ~

a piay on the word alien — the fo-
cal point of this town of 100. “I think
our government is working in con-
junction with them.”

“I don't doubt for a minute that
there are extraterrestrials,” said
Chuck Clark, an amateur astrono-
mer who moved here 14 years ago
and has written a guidebook on the
area. “To think we're the only life
in the universe is ludicrous.”

Area 51 is steeped in mystery.
Here the government has tested
exotic aircraft udtime-the-L1.2,

Blackbird, F-117A Stealth’

ter and now the top-secret Au-
Tora, said to be the succesor to the
high-flying Blackbird reconnais-

The military has refused to ac-
knowledge the existence of a base
in the heavily guarded area 85
miles northwest of Las Vegas.

UFO buffs say an alien found in
the crash of a spaceship near Ros-
weil, N.M., on July 8, 1947, was tak-
en to Area 51.

The Nevada Department of
Transportation, mindful of the
growing interest in this remote
area, Tecently named the 92-mile
stretch of state Route 375 the Ex-
traterrestrial Highway.

Special road signs are planned
for this desolate stretch, which
draws about 50 vehicles a day.

Gov. Robert Miller suggested,
tongue-in-cheek, that some signs
for the Extraterrestrial Highway be
placed flat on the ground “so aliens
can land there.”

Miller said the new name shows
Nevada has a sense of humor, as
was the case several years ago

u

“and Sun. §6 Pas Fac. Charge addni

STATE

_.wuunters

Photos by Bob Macy. AP

Highway 375 Some people in Rachel, Nev., are convinced that extraterrestrial visitors live just over the mountains.

“There are people trom other planets over there’: Pat Travis and her
husband, Joe, sell UFO-related T-shirts, caps and books at their desert cafe.

When a magazine named Highway

“Instead of being insulted, we
turned {t around, set up way sta-
tions, and created T-shirts and
bumper stickers reading, ‘I sur-
vived the loneHest road in Ameri-
ca,'" Miller said.

The state will erect four 3-foot-
by-8-foot Extraterrestrial Highway
signs this year.

The highway runs between the
tiny hamlets of Hiko and Warm
Springs, across terrain ranging
from three mountain passes to des-
ert decked with scrub brush and
Juniper trees. The town of Rachel
consists of a cluster of mobile
homes, a gas station and the Little
A'Le’inn, a bar and cafe run by
Travis and her husband, Joe.

On a recent morning, Pat Travis
was tending to the kitchen, catch-
ing her breath from an invasion of
Marines who stop by daily for
breakfast before heading to duty at
‘a nearby restricted military area.

Were they going to Area 51?

Where, ther?

“Can't say,” she replied.

Pat Travis said several people
who live in Rachel work at Area 51,
or the Tonopah Test Range to the
north, where the F-117A was based
while it was still a classified project
She said the workers, military and
civilian, are closemouthed.

“T have never had anybody who
works at Area 5) tell us anything,”
she said. “We've had some of them
get pretty drunk and they still don't
tell anything.”

Clark, 50, said he’s witnessed
mysterious sights such as “glowing
orbs of light” around Area 51.

Many have made similar sight-
ings in this haven for UFO buffs,
Clark said. “The stuff that is being
seen is alien, but under the control

’ of our government,” he said.

The tiny cafe sells UFO-related
T-shirts, caps and books, Its walls
are lined with pictures of extrater-
restrial types and photos taken
from a distance of the hangars and
30,000-foot runway at Groom Lake.

The photos were taken before
the government last year banned
access to two bluffs overlooking the
Groom Lake complex.

UFO fans still seek out the black
mailbox alongside Highway 375
that marks the road leading to re-
stricted land around Area 51.
‘Armed guards keep gawkers more
than 7 miles from the area.

But they cannot block the sights
and sounds, Clark says, such as the

sky. “It's louder than anything I've
ever heard,” Joe Travis said.

working in Tonopah, helping build
the F-117A base.

Pat Travis denied that the UFO
stories are a ploy to sell paraphei
alia at her tiny cafe, “I really be-
lieve in UFOs," she said, flipping a
Pancake on a griddle.

Pat Travis, 52, sald once a
strange beam of light pierced an
iron door at the cafe. She has seen
many strange sights in the night-
time sky, too, but has never encoun-
tered an alien being, she said.

Same for Joe Travis, 57. But he
says he knows why: “I've heard if
you smoke and drink, they won't
have anything to do with you,” the
Tuddy-faced, bearded Travis said,
puffing on a cigarette while
perched on a stoo! at the cafe's bar,

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. Received a call from Es. = Last

Saturday morning (22 Feb), at 0215 hours, she and a friend were
driving home north of Peebles and they saw two or three
distinctive very bright lights in a field, hovering.

2. I explained the MOD position on "UFOs" and offered to let
her know if any explanation came to light for the sighting.

She clearly understood that we were not necessarily going to be
able to come up with an explanation, but did not think what she
saw was "extraterrestrial". She was just interes@to know if
someone else had seen it and could say what it was.

UNUSUAL EVENT —_REECRTED PY i faa
Section 40 #2MESection 40

1. At about 22.00-22.i5hrs on Sunday, 25 Feb 96, Mrs
BEESON. accompanied by her 29yr old daughter, was driving
south from Aberystwyth on the A487 road. About half way
between Blaenplwyf and Llanrhystud they saw a large pale
(whiteish) light in the 11 o’clock position at an angle of 30-
45% from the horizontal. The light was diffused or "watered
down" and most unusual. It moved slowly from left to right
across their path.

2. Shortly afterwards several white lights appeared from the
area of the first sighting and proceeded to approach the car,
passing over it very, very low ("skimmed the car") from right
to left. These appeared to "become" an elliptical white light
behind hills to the left of them which eventually went out.

As the lights crossed the car the observers were looking up
at, perhaps, an angle of 80°.

3. seemed a very sensible and responsible person
who does not believe in UFOs. However, neither she nor her
daughter have ever seen anything like this before and, if we
have an explanation, they would be interested to know.

4. The weather was cloudy at the time. Peay ‘s
telephone number is Seedy . We have had no other

reports of such sightings to date and I find it difficult to
imagine the cause of this.

RAF CRO (Wales)

UNIDENTIFIED FLYING OBJECT

In accordance with our local instructions, 1 forward details of a reported UFO at Hatfield Peverel,
Essex on Wednesday 15 November 1995. The incident reference number is IRIS 963 15.11.95.
Please contact us should you require any further information.

a) 15.11.95 2315 hours

b) One ‘enormous’ flat oval shaped object - could see no lights or markings other than
extreme bright light which shone to the ground. Pulsating/vibrating noise but not that of a
jet engine.

c} Section 40| FRan standing in field at rear of the Scout hut in Church Road, Hatfield
Peverel (Grid Ref 5794 2113)

d) Naked eye

e) As (c)

f) Directly above

8) As (f)

h} Did not hang around to see!

i) Clear night sky

n) On being spoken to, SEMICON was clearly petrified of what she had seen - refused at
the time to return to location because of her fear.

o) None

1. The following Unidentified Flying Object (UFO) report has been received at this Unit, today 4p¥
2 October 1995. ay

—

WESTON-SUPER-MARE

|
Time/Date: Throughout Period 2355 29 September 1995 - 0010 30 September 1995.
Description: Visibility Good - clear starlit night.

UFO appearance - "Inverted ‘Soup Plate! with clearly defined apertures”.
UFO followed overhead a vehicle being driven from Cheddar via
Axbridge - Lower Weare - A38 - Lympsham - Weston-super-Mare.

UFO seen to move at speed to left of vehicle and return overhead getting
progressively lower. UFO last seen at outskirts to Weston-super-Mare at
General Hospital area.

2. is adamant that this was not a helicopter and states that she feels foolish in reporting
the incident, however such is her conviction of the incident,considered that it should be reported.

REPORT OF UFO SIGHTING 45 / SZ

Date: Friday, 25 August 1995.
Time: 2200 - 2230 Local.
Location: Latimer and Chesham, Buckinghamshire.

Reported by: Rimi

Chesham, Bucks. Tel: Geto .

SERENE said that she was travelling in a car being driven by
her daughter, RRMMME, with her two grandchildren. As they were
approaching Latimer from Chorley Wood her daughter noticed some
lights above the car. thought that they may be
reflections of spotlights from a disco and they stopped the car
to see if they could hear the music. It was completely silent.
EECMOMEEN made out a disc in the sky with a number of vivid
green lights around the sides and she was completely mystified.
As they dreve off along a country lane towards Chesham, about 5
or 6 miles away, they could see the object apparently following
them. When the car slowed the object appeared to slow as well,
but on one occasion it appeared to come alongeide. At this point
she estimates that it may have been about 100 metres away but she
was not able to give an estimate of its aize. They stopped the
car to have a better look and two beams of light seemed to appear
from beneath the object towards the ground. They drove off again
and it was still visible as they entered the residential area of
Chesham, although it subsequently disappeared.

SEMIRMMMMNN Said she had been plucking up scurage tc tell someone
about what she saw and that was why it had taken several weeks
to do sc. She seemed to be somewhat embarrassed and feared that
she would be dismissed as a crackpot. I think she genuinely
believes that she saw something inexplicable that night.

Section40,
HALIFAX, W YORKS.

Sir, ] wish to report the following marginally named incident concerning
the alleged sighting of an unidentified cbject above the informants’
home address at 2250 hours on Saturday, 15 Juty 1995,
The concerned informants in this incident are as fallows,

1. Section 40)

2. Section 40

3. Section 40

All persons concerned were having a barbecue at the rear of their semi-
detached dwelling when they describe a UFO type object seemed to just
suddenly appear from nowhere approximately thirty feet away from the
back door, hovering only a matter of feet above the adjacent bungalow
that faces onto their garden area. It was established the viewing only
lasted for five minutes in Which time the object initially hovered, but
then went on to make erratic jerky movements at the same height

moving towards Sowerby Bridge.

ebO° aoed NoOLARAT LS3M Malolos tlist Se. War Fs

WEST YORKSHIRE POLICE

Both informants 1 and 2 state the object did not come from either left
or right, but seemed to come down out of nowhere. The actual object
is described in the following manner - shape, that of a Satum like ball
with an extended ring around its circumference. In addition, Miss
SERINE states it had what appeared to be red portholes and distinct
four blue lights as per the below diagram. They both stated no noise or
sound originated from the object, which seemed to light only the rear

garden area,
There is rto indication of any obstructions or evidence it looked like
It is clear all informants have been shocked by the experience and I

formed the impression their account was genuine and consistent,
although the content was somewhat bizarre.

Lhave not received at this stage any similar occurrences or sightings and
therefore submit this report for your attention.

DETAILS OF OCCURRENCE

1. QGESRZOM who claimed to be ex-RAFP (REMMI) and also to have

left the RAF in 1984 stated that he had seen what he thought was a UFO
over Poole Harbour at 0415 hours this a.m.

2. He described it as Silver in colour with bright lights and sounded
like a helicopter on the ground({ a low, slow beating sound). He gave
no description of height or direction of movement.

3. He had not reported this occurrence to the Civil Police.

Notes:

1. Members of the public who submit reports shouid be informed that their

reports have heen passed to the Ministry of Defence,

2. Sightings by Service personnel and action taken as a resuit of civilian
sightings must not be disclosed to members of the Press who, if they make
enquiries, should be referred to the Ministry of Defence.

3. A routine unclassified signal, or Fax (SEQMMOME) should be sent to
Ministry of Defence, Londen (for the attention of MOD Sec(AS)2A). The format
of the signal is as set out in the report above. The signal SIC is Z6F.

4, Contact MOD Sec(AS)2a as soon as possible at MOD Main Building Ext 82140
